What connection the high percentage of suicide attacks executed by Islamist groups since 1980 has to do with the religion of Islam is disputed. Specifically, scholars, researchers, and others disagree over whether Islam forbids suicide in the process of attacking enemies, or the killing of civilians. According to a report compiled by the Chicago Project on Suicide Terrorism, 224 of 300 suicide terror attacks from 1980 to 2003 involved Islamist groups or took place in Muslim-majority countries.[^cite_note-546] Another tabulation found more than a fourfold increase in suicide bombings in the two years following Pape's study and that the overwhelming majority of these bombers were motivated by the ideology of Islamist martyrdom.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEAtran2006131-133-528] For example, as of early 2008, 1,121 Muslim suicide bombers have blown themselves up in [Iraq](7515928.md).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEFisk2008-547]

##### Recent perpetration of suicide attacks by Muslims

[Sunni Muslims](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sunni_Muslims) were possibly the last major branch of the [Abrahamic religions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abrahamic_religions) to resort to overt suicide attacks. Islamic suicide bombing is a fairly recent phenomenon. It was absent from the 1979–1989 [Afghan jihad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soviet%E2%80%93Afghan_War) against the Soviet Union,[^cite_note-FeldmanITNA-311] an asymmetrical war where the *mujahideen* fought [Soviet warplanes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soviet_Air_Forces), helicopters and tanks primarily with light weapons. According to author [Sadakat Kadri](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sadakat_Kadri), "the very idea that Muslims might blow themselves up for God was unheard of before 1983, and it was not until the early 1990s that anyone anywhere had tried to justify killing innocent Muslims who were not on a battlefield". After 1983, the process was limited among Muslims to [Hezbollah](13919.md) and other [Lebanese Shia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lebanese_Shia_Muslims) factions for more than a decade.[^cite_note-Kadri-312]

Since then, according to [Noah Feldman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Noah_Feldman), videotaped pre-confession of faith by attackers known as the "vocabulary of martyrdom and sacrifice" have become part of "Islamic cultural consciousness" and these confessions are "instantly recognizable" to Muslims.[^cite_note-FeldmanITNA-311] The tactic has spread through the Muslim world "with astonishing speed and on a surprising course".[^cite_note-FeldmanITNA-311] "First the targets were American soldiers, then mostly Israelis, including women and children. From [Lebanon](17771.md) and Israel, the technique of suicide bombing moved to Iraq, where the targets have included mosques and shrines, and the intended victims have mostly been [Shia](26961.md) [Iraqis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iraqi_people). ... \[In] [Afghanistan](737.md), ... both the perpetrators and the targets are orthodox [Sunni](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sunni) Muslims. Not long ago, a bombing in Lashkar Gah, the capital of [Helmand Province](457235.md), killed Muslims, including women, who were applying to go on [pilgrimage](23487999.md) to [Mecca](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mecca). Overall, the trend is definitively in the direction of Muslim-on-Muslim violence. By a conservative accounting, more than three times as many Iraqis have been killed by suicide bombings in just three years (2003–6) as have Israelis in ten (from 1996 to 2006). Suicide bombing has become the archetype of Muslim violence;– not just to Westerners but also to Muslims themselves".[^cite_note-FeldmanITNA-311]

Recent research on the rationale of suicide bombing has identified both religious and sociopolitical motivations.[^cite_note-autogenerated2002-548][^cite_note-549] Those who cite religious factors as an important influence note that religion provides the framework because the bombers believe they are acting in the name of Islam and will be [rewarded as martyrs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martyrdom_in_Islam). Since martyrdom is seen as a step towards paradise, those who commit suicide while discarding their community from a common enemy believe that they will reach an ultimate salvation after they die.[^cite_note-autogenerated2002-548]

In the media attention given to suicide bombing during the [Second Intifada](71717.md) and after 9/11, sources hostile to radical Islamism quoted radical scholars promising various heavenly rewards, such as 70 virgins ([Arabic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language): *[houri](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Houri)*) as wives, to Muslims who die as martyrs, specifically as suicide attackers.[^cite_note-550][^cite_note-551] Other alleged rewards for those dying are being cleansed of all sin and brought directly to paradise, and not having to wait for the [Day of Judgement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Last_Judgment#Islam).[^cite_note-552][^cite_note-553]

Others, such as [As'ad AbuKhalil](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/As'ad_AbuKhalil), maintain that "the tendency to dwell on the sexual motives" of the suicide bombers "belittles" the bombers "sociopolitical causes", and that the alleged "sexual frustration" of young Muslim men "has been overly emphasized in the Western and Israeli media" as a motive for terrorism.[^cite_note-klare-7-11-2001-533] [Suicide is criminalised](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suicide_legislation) many in majority-Muslim countries ([see above](#suicide)).

##### Support for "martyrdom operations" and analogous concepts

Further information: [Martyrdom § Islam](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martyrdom#Islam), [Martyrdom in Islam](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martyrdom_in_Islam), [Suicide legislation § Asia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suicide_legislation#Asia), and [Islamic view of death § Suicide](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islamic_view_of_death#Suicide)

Islamist militant organizations including [al-Qaeda](1921.md), [Hamas](13913.md), and [Palestinian Islamic Jihad](13093253.md) argue that, despite what some Muslims claim is Islam's strict prohibition of suicide and murder,[^cite_note-554][^cite_note-555] suicide attacks fulfill the obligation of *[jihad](16203.md)* against the "oppressor", "martyrs" will be rewarded with paradise, and have the support of some Muslim clerics. Clerics have supported suicide attacks largely in connection with the Palestinian issue. Prominent Sunni cleric [Yusuf al-Qaradawi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yusuf_al-Qaradawi) had previously supported such attacks by Palestinians in perceived defense of their homeland as heroic and an act of resistance.[^cite_note-556] [Shia Lebanese](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lebanese_Shia_Muslims) cleric [Muhammad Husayn Fadlallah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muhammad_Husayn_Fadlallah), the spiritual authority recognized by Hezbollah, holds similar views.[^cite_note-FeldmanITNA-311]

The articles maintains that [Abu Huraira](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abu_Huraira), a companion of the Muhammad, and [Umar ibn Khattab](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Umar_ibn_Khattab), the second caliph of Islam, approved acts which Muslims knew would lead to certain death. The Islamic prophet Muhammad also approved of such acts, according to authors Maulana Muawiya Hussaini and Ikrimah Anwar who cited numerous [Hadith](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hadith) of Muhammad on the authority of Islamic jurist [Muslim ibn al-Hajjaj](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muslim_ibn_al-Hajjaj). "The Sahaba \[companions of the Islamic prophet Muhammad] who carried out the attacks almost certainly knew that they were going to be killed during their operations but they still carried them out and such acts were extolled and praised in the sharia."[^cite_note-557]

#### Opposition and responses from Muslim scholars

Others, such as Middle East historian [Bernard Lewis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bernard_Lewis), disagree: "... a clear difference was made between throwing oneself to certain death at the hands of an overwhelmingly strong enemy, and dying by one's own hand. The first, if conducted in a properly authorized \[ [jihad](16203.md) ], was a passport to heaven; the second to damnation. The blurring of their previously vital distinction was the work of some twentieth-century theologians who outlined the new theory which the suicide bombers put into practice".[^cite_note-ARSI-BL-xii-109]

The distinction from engaging in an act where the perpetrator plans to fight to the death but where the attack does not require their death is important to at least one Islamist terror group, [Lashkar-e-Taiba](649718.md) (LeT). While the group extols "martyrdom" and has killed many civilians, LeT believes suicide attacks where the attackers die by their own hand, such as by pressing a detonation button, are *[haram](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Haram)* (forbidden). Its "trademark" is that of perpetrators fighting "to the death" but escaping "if practical". "This distinction has been the subject of extensive discourse among radical Islamist leaders".[^cite_note-558]

Several Western and Muslim scholars of Islam have posited that suicide attacks are a clear violation of classical Islamic law, and characterized such attacks against civilians as murderous and sinful.[^cite_note-CNN-559][^cite_note-Lewis_2008,_p53-560]

According to [Bernard Lewis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bernard_Lewis), "the emergence of the now widespread terrorism practice of suicide bombing is a development of the 20th century. It has no antecedents in Islamic history, and no justification in terms of Islamic theology, law, or tradition."[^cite_note-Lewis_2008,_p53-560] Islamic legal [rules of armed warfare](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rules_of_war_in_Islam) or military *jihad* are covered in detail in the classical texts of Islamic jurisprudence,[^cite_note-FeldmanITNA-311] which forbid the killing of women, children, or non-combatants, and the destruction of cultivated or residential areas.[^cite_note-FeldmanITNA-311][^cite_note-LewisChurchill-561][^cite_note-562]

For more than a millennium, these tenets were accepted by Sunnis and Shiites. However, since the 1980s militant Islamists have challenged the traditional Islamic rules of warfare to justify suicide attacks.[^cite_note-FeldmanITNA-311][^cite_note-LewisChurchill-561]

Several respected Muslim scholars have provided scholastic refutations of suicide bombings, condemning them as terrorism prohibited in Islam and leading their perpetrators to hell.[^cite_note-CNN-559] In his over 400 page long *[Fatwa on Terrorism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fatwa_on_Terrorism)* condemning suicide attacks, Muslim Islamic scholar Muhammad [Tahir-ul-Qadri](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tahir-ul-Qadri) directly disputed the rationale of Islamists. He argues that indiscriminately killing both Muslims and non-Muslims is unlawful, and brings the Muslim *[ummah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ummah)* into disrepute, no matter how lofty the killers intentions.[^cite_note-563] [Tahir-ul-Qadri](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tahir-ul-Qadri) states terrorism "has no place in Islamic teaching, and no justification can be provided to it \[...] good intention cannot justify a wrong and forbidden act".[^cite_note-CNN-559]

[Grand Mufti](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grand_Mufti_of_Saudi_Arabia) [Saudi Arabia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terrorism_in_Saudi_Arabia), [Abdul-Aziz ibn Abdullah Al Shaykh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abdul-Aziz_ibn_Abdullah_Al_Shaykh), issued a *[fatwa](11259.md)* on 12 September 2013 that suicide bombings are "great crimes" and bombers are "criminals who rush themselves to hell by their actions". Al Shaykh described suicide bombers as "robbed of their minds \[...] who have been used \[as tools] to destroy themselves and societies".[^cite_note-564] "In view of the fast-moving dangerous developments in the Islamic world, it is very distressing to see the tendencies of permitting or underestimating the shedding of blood of Muslims and those under protection in their countries. The sectarian or ignorant utterances made by some of these people would benefit none other than the greedy, vindictive and envious people. Hence, we would like to draw attention to the seriousness of the attacks on Muslims or those who live under their protection or under a pact with them|Al Shaykh, quoting a number of verses from the Qur'an and Hadith".[^cite_note-565] Even countries that have a history of suicide attacks, and have regarded them as martyrdom, have condemned them in situations they see as illegitimate.

Following the [2005 Bangladesh bombings](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2005_Bangladesh_bombings) by the banned outfit [Jama'atul Mujahideen Bangladesh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jama'atul_Mujahideen_Bangladesh) (JMB), chief cleric of [Bangladesh](3454.md) [Ubaidul Haq](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ubaidul_Haq) led a protest of *[ulema](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ulema)* denouncing [terrorism](30636.md).[^cite_note-566] He said: "[Islam](6037917.md) prohibits [suicide bombings](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suicide_bombings). These bombers are enemies of Islam. \[...] It is a duty for all [Muslims](19541.md) to stand up against those who are killing people in the name of [Islam](6037917.md)".

In January 2006, [Shia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shi'a_Islam) [Marja](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marja') (high ranking cleric) [Ayatollah al-Udhma Yousof al-Sanei](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yousef_Sanei) decreed a *[fatwa](11259.md)* against suicide bombing, declaring it a "terrorist act".[^cite_note-FeldmanITNA-311][^cite_note-567] In 2005, Muhammad Afifi al-Akiti also issued a *fatwa* "Against The Targeting Of Civilians".[^cite_note-568]

American based Islamic jurist and scholar [Khaled Abou Al-Fadl](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Khaled_Abou_Al-Fadl) argues, "The classical jurists, nearly without exception, argued that those who attack by stealth, while targeting noncombatants in order to terrorize the resident and wayfarer, are corrupters of the earth. "Resident and wayfarer" was a legal expression that meant that whether the attackers terrorize people in their urban centers or terrorize travelers, the result was the same: all such attacks constitute a corruption of the earth. The legal term given to people who act this way was *muharibun* (those who wage war against society), and the crime is called the crime of *hiraba* (waging war against society). The crime of *hiraba* was so serious and repugnant that, according to Islamic law, those guilty of this crime were considered enemies of humankind and were not to be given quarter or sanctuary anywhere .... Those who are familiar with the classical tradition will find the parallels between what were described as crimes of *hiraba* and what is often called terrorism today nothing short of remarkable. The classical jurists considered crimes such as assassinations, setting fires, or poisoning water wells – that could indiscriminately kill the innocent – as offenses of *hiraba*. Furthermore, hijacking methods of transportation or crucifying people in order to spread fear are also crimes of *hiraba*. Importantly, Islamic law strictly prohibited the taking of hostages, the mutilation of corpses, and torture".[^cite_note-569]

According to *[When Religion Becomes Evil](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/When_Religion_Becomes_Evil)*, by [Baptist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baptist) minister [Charles Kimball](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charles_Kimball_\(minister\)?action=edit\&redlink=1), "There is only one verse in the Qur'an that contains a phrase related to suicide" (4\:29).[^cite_note-570] "[O](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/%D9%8A%D8%A7) you who have believed, do not consume one another's wealth unjustly but only \[in lawful] business by mutual consent. And do not kill yourselves. Indeed, Allah is to you ever Merciful".[^cite_note-571]

Some commentators posit that "do not kill yourselves" is better translated "do not kill each other", and some translations, such as those by M. H. Shakir, reflect that view. Mainstream Islamic groups such as the [European Council for Fatwa and Research](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Council_for_Fatwa_and_Research) also cite the Quranic verse [Al-An'am](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-An'am) 6\:151[^cite_note-572]) as prohibiting suicide: "And take not life, which Allah has made sacred, except by way of justice and law".[^cite_note-573] The *[Hadith](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hadith)*, including Bukhari 2\:445, states: "The Prophet said, '...whoever commits suicide with a piece of iron will be punished with the same piece of iron in the Hell Fire', \[and] 'A man was inflicted with wounds and he committed suicide, and so Allah said: 'My slave has caused death on himself hurriedly, so I forbid Paradise for him'".[^cite_note-574][^cite_note-575]

Other Muslims have also noted Quranic verses in opposition to suicide, to taking of life other than by way of justice such as the death penalty for murder, and to collective punishment.[^cite_note-576]

The international community considers the use of [indiscriminate attacks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indiscriminate_attack) on civilian populations[^cite_note-rwkckb07-363][^cite_note-577] as [illegal under international law](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Geneva_conventions#Protocols).[^cite_note-578]

##### From Muslims in Muslim-minority countries

There are also objections from Muslim minority countries. Israel – a [Jewish state](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_state) with a [Muslim minority](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islam_in_Israel) – has also glorified [militant](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_terrorism) [martyrdom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martyrdom_in_Judaism) in educational materials and political propaganda, particularly [Likud](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Likud_video_of_graves), the political successor organisation to two [Zionist militant](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zionist_militant) groups ([Menachem Begin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Menachem_Begin)'s [Irgun](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Irgun) and [Yitzhak Shamir](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yitzhak_Shamir)'s [Lehi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lehi_\(militant_group\))).[^cite_note-yahu's_speech-579][^cite_note-study_unit-58] In 2010 Likud introduced a new study unit to the [school curriculum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Education_in_Israel) that focused on [martyred militants](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Olei_Hagardom) from these groups, including two who [intentionally killed themselves](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suicide_mission#1947) with explosives.[^cite_note-yahu's_speech-579][^cite_note-Charters_2007-238][^cite_note-Sheleg_2007_suicide_operation-55] The most passionate objections to this glorification of [terrorist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_terrorism) [martyrdom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martyrdom_in_Judaism) came from two Muslim [Knesset](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Knesset_members) members, [Ahmed Tibi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ahmed_Tibi) and [Talab El-Sana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Talab_El-Sana), both from the Islmist faction [United Arab List](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Arab_List)-Ta'a.[^cite_note-Tibi_ejected-580] Some Jewish members also objected, including by comparing Netanyahu to Hamas.[^cite_note-Tibi_ejected-580]

Ihsanic Intelligence, a London-based Islamic think-tank, published their two-year study into suicide bombings in the name of Islam titled *The Hijacked Caravan*.[^cite_note-581] The study concluded that, "The technique of suicide bombing is anathema, antithetical and abhorrent to Sunni Islam. It is considered legally forbidden, constituting a reprehensible innovation in the Islamic tradition, morally an enormity of sin combining suicide and murder and theologically an act which has consequences of eternal damnation".[^cite_note-582]

[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEPedahzur20048-15]: &#x20;[Pedahzur 2004](#CITEREFPedahzur2004), p. 8.

[^cite_note-ICT-STDC-16]: &#x20;[Yoram Schweitzer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yoram_Schweitzer) (21 April 2000). ["Suicide Terrorism: Development and Characteristics"](https://web.archive.org/web/20130527191602/http://www.ict.org.il/Articles/tabid/66/Articlsid/42/Default.aspx). [International Institute for Counter-Terrorism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_Institute_for_Counter-Terrorism). Archived from [the original](http://www.ict.org.il/Articles/tabid/66/Articlsid/42/Default.aspx) on 27 May 2013. Retrieved 22 March 2015. \[a suicide terror attack is] a very specific kind of attack. It does not deal with the very high-risk terror operations that leave only little chance of survival to their perpetrators. Such attacks as the [Japanese Red Army](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Japanese_Red_Army)'s (JRA) attack at Lod airport in 1972, [Abu Nidal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abu_Nidal)'s attack on a synagogue in Istanbul in 1986 and the [PFLP-GC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PFLP-GC) hand-glider attack on an army barracks in [Kiryat Shmona](323369.md) in 1987 fall outside the scope of this paper. Also excluded were the self-inflicted deaths of members of terrorist organization … a politically motivated violent attack perpetrated by a self-aware individual (or individuals) who actively and purposely causes his own death through blowing himself up along with his chosen target … the perpetrator's ensured death is a precondition for the success of his mission.

[^cite_note-Dodd-17]: &#x20;Dodd, Henry (23 August 2013). [*A short history of suicide bombing*](https://web.archive.org/web/20140125110530/https://aoav.org.uk/2013/a-short-history-of-suicide-bombings/). Action on Armed Violence. Archived from [the original](https://aoav.org.uk/2013/a-short-history-of-suicide-bombings/) on 25 January 2014. Retrieved 6 October 2015. First of all let's be clear what kind of attacks we are talking about. Suicide bombings are those that involve the deliberate death of the perpetrator. We're not just talking about a reckless charge in battle. The focus is on those attacks where the perpetrator functions as a sophisticated guidance system for the weapon. They function as part human and part weapon. In this way they are suicide attacks rather than suicidal attacks.

[^cite_note-LANKFORD-17-12-2012-18]: &#x20;Lankford, Adam (17 December 2012). ["What Drives Suicidal Mass Killers"](https://www.nytimes.com/2012/12/18/opinion/what-drives-suicidal-mass-killers.html). *[The New York Times](30680.md)*. Retrieved 7 October 2015. For years, the conventional wisdom has been that suicide terrorists are rational political actors, while suicidal rampage shooters are mentally disturbed loners. But the two groups have far more in common than has been recognized.... Although suicide terrorists may share the same beliefs as the organizations whose propaganda they spout, they are primarily motivated by the desire to kill and be killed — just like most rampage shooters.

The two young men who shot and bombed their way through their high school here planned the assault a year in advance, monitored cafeteria crowds to determine the busiest times and scheduled **their suicide attack** for Hitler's birthday, the authorities said today, citing a handwritten diary. \[Emphasis added.]

Archived from [the original](https://aja.me/82vcg1) on 7 January 2026. وقالت مصادر محلية إن "الانتحاري" كان مع شخص آخر يعتزمان التسلل عبر منطقة الشريط الحدودي إلى سيناء المصرية، ولدى توقيفه من الأمن فجر نفسه، مما أدى إلى مقتله وإصابة مرافقه. \[Local sources said that the "suicide bomber" was with another person who intended to infiltrate across the border strip area into the Egyptian Sinai, and when he was stopped by security, he blew himself up, which led to his death and the injury of his companion.]

[^cite_note-anger_over_deviant_ideology-41]: &#x20;["Targeting border guards: Public anger over deviant ideology" استهداف حماة الثغور.. غضب شعبي من الفكر المنحرف](https://web.archive.org/web/20260107180940/https://palinfo.com/news/2017/08/17/56428/). *palinfo.com* (in Arabic). 17 August 2017. Archived from [the original](https://palinfo.com/news/2017/08/17/56428/) on 7 January 2026. حيث حذّر الخبير الأمني إبراهيم حبيب، من رد الفعل المتطرف على الحادثة، وقال: "برغم خطورة الحدث في رفح، وبرغم استشهاد أحد عناصر الضبط الميداني الأبطال وجرح العديد منهم، وما لهذا الحادث من دلالات أمنية خطيرة، إلا أنني أدعو صنّاع القرار في غزة بالتروي وعدم الاستعجال في اتخاذ قرارات قد تؤدي إلى فتح مواجهة جديدة داخل القطاع مع هذه العناصر المنحرفة". \[Security expert Ibrahim Habib warned against an extreme reaction to the incident, saying: "Despite the seriousness of the event in Rafah, and despite the martyrdom of one of the heroic field control officers and the wounding of many others, and the serious security implications of this incident, I call on decision-makers in Gaza to be deliberate and not rush into taking decisions that may lead to opening a new confrontation within the sector with these deviant elements".]

[^cite_note-kikar-51]: &#x20;[שלושה אירועי "תָּמוֹת נַפְשִׁי עִם פְּלִשְׁתִּים" של גיבורינו בעזה](https://www.kikar.co.il/haredim-news/s8s5o8). *כיכר השבת [www.kikar.co.il](http://www.kikar.co.il/)* (in Hebrew). 13 February 2024. כתב ההיסטוריון צבי אילן כי במהלך הקרב הקשה הוצת זחל"מ ישראלי, ונהגו ביצע מעשה הקרבה בל יאמן. הוא הסיע את הרכב הבוער לעבר שני זחל"מים מצריים, התנגש בהם ופוצץ אותם על יושביהם. מהדורה זו היא שלישית במספר של "תמות נפשי עם פלישתים", וההיסטוריה חוזרת על עצמה שוב ושוב בגבורת העם היהודי הנאבק באויביו.

[^cite_note-yNet_Ali-52]: &#x20;Shakib Ali ([Arabic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language): شكيب علي) [Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): שקיב (שכיב) עלי (8 December 2023). [לשחרר אותם לפני שלחמאס לא יהיה מה להפסיד](https://www.ynet.co.il/news/article/r1qcbdeit). *Ynet* (in Hebrew). מצמררת המחשבה על הסיפור המקראי על שמשון הגיבור, שנתפס על ידי הפלישתים והוחזק בעזה, שם נקשר לעמודי המקדש. אחרי שהבין שלא יוכל להשתחרר, העדיף לנקום באויביו ומוטט את עמודי המבנה עליו ועל שוביו ואמר "תמות נפשי עם פלישתים".

[^cite_note-Judges_16-53]: &#x20;From [Judges 16](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Judges_16)\:30 – Usually translated as&#x20;*&#x20;'Let me die with the Philistines'&#x20;*&#x20;– ["Judges 16: Hebrew - English Bible - Mechon-Mamre"](https://mechon-mamre.org/p/pt/pt0716.htm). *mechon-mamre.org*. **English:** 30 And Samson said: 'Let me die with the Philistines'. And he bent with all his might; and the house fell upon the lords, and upon all the people that were therein. So the dead that he slew at his death were more than they that he slew in his life. **Hebrew:** ל וַיֹּאמֶר שִׁמְשׁוֹן, תָּמוֹת נַפְשִׁי עִם-פְּלִשְׁתִּים, וַיֵּט בְּכֹחַ, וַיִּפֹּל הַבַּיִת עַל-הַסְּרָנִים וְעַל-כָּל-הָעָם אֲשֶׁר-בּוֹ; וַיִּהְיוּ הַמֵּתִים, אֲשֶׁר הֵמִית בְּמוֹתוֹ, רַבִּים, מֵאֲשֶׁר הֵמִית בְּחַיָּיו.

[^cite_note-rev_globes-54]: &#x20;(Author name in [Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): דרור פויר) Dror Feuer (26 January 2009). [תמות נפשי עם פלישתים](https://www.globes.co.il/news/article.aspx?did=1000419938) \[Let my soul die with the Philistines]. *[Globes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Globes_\(newspaper\)) [www.globes.co.il](http://www.globes.co.il/)*. שבצה"ל החמירו את נוהל "חניבעל" והנחו את החיילים למנוע בכל מחיר מקרה של "גלעד שליט 2". כלומר: אם אתה חייל ומנסים לחטוף אותך, עליך להתנגד. לא הולך? עליך להתאבד ולקחת כמה שיותר לוחמי חמאס אתך (מג"ד מגולני ממליץ על שימוש ברימון אישי…) … אם זהו הלך המחשבה, למה שלא יקחו את נוהל "חניבעל" וישדרגו אותו עוד קצת ויהפכו אותו לנוהל "שמשון"? למה לחכות שיבואו חוטפים כשאפשר לקחת את החיילים ולשלוח אותם ישר להתאבד, בבחינת תמות נפשי עם פלשתים, ולסגור עניין. \[(machine translation) The IDF strictened the "Hannibal" procedure and instructed the soldiers to prevent a case of "[Gilad Shalit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gilad_Shalit) 2" at all costs. In other words: if you are a soldier and they try to kidnap you, you must resist. Le Holech? (see note) You must commit suicide and take as many Hamas fighters with you as possible (the battalion commander from Golani recommends the use of a grenade) ... If this is the mindset, why not take the "Hannibal" procedure and upgrade it a little further and turn it into the "[Shimshon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shimshon#Suicide_attack)" procedure? Why wait for kidnappers to come when it is possible to take the soldiers and send them straight to commit suicide, in the sense of [dying with the Philistines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Die_with_the_Philistines), and closing the matter.]`{{cite news}}`: CS1 maint: deprecated archival service ([link](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Category\:CS1_maint:_deprecated_archival_service)) **Note:** the phrase "Le Holech?" (לא הולך?) can be translated as *"not working?"* or *"won't you?"* or a few other phrases.&#x20;

[^cite_note-Sheleg_2007_suicide_operation-55]: &#x20;[Sheleg 2007](#CITEREFSheleg2007) (Yoram Tamir director of the [Museum of Underground Prisoners](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Museum_of_Underground_Prisoners)) "…says the Lehi had envisioned a suicide operation during the hanging of one of their men prior to this incident: "They called it Operation Samson, in an allusion to the suicide of the biblical figure." Eliezer Ben-Ami, who prepared the makeshift orange grenades while he was imprisoned along with the two men, confirms that the plan was to turn their ascent to the gallows into an action that would harm the British authorities."

[^cite_note-murdered_by_the_British-56]: &#x20;[Begin 1947](#CITEREFBegin1947) harvnb error: no target: CITEREFBegin1947 ([help](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Category\:Harv_and_Sfn_template_errors)): [Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): הם לא איבדו את עצמם לדעת. הם נרצחו על-ידי התליין הבריטי., [lit.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Literal_translation) 'They did not commit the sin of suicide; they were murdered by the British executioner.'

[^cite_note-Sheleg_2007_approval-57]: &#x20;[Sheleg 2007](#CITEREFSheleg2007) "Of course, we needed the condemned men's approval," (the Lehi veteran) recalls. "Moshe agreed right away, but since there was an [Irgun](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Irgun) man with him we had to request their approval, too. We asked the person responsible for Irgun prisoners in the jail, [Yehoshua Tamler](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eliyahu_Tamler), what he thought, and he said they needed the consent of the top command. We had to wait a few days, despite fearing that they would be taken to the gallows in the meantime, until approval arrived from the commander of the Irgun, [Menachem Begin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Menachem_Begin)."

[^cite_note-Nassau-61]: &#x20;["'Human Bomb' Kills Self, Wounds Wife"](https://web.archive.org/web/20251006033828/https://www.nyshistoricnewspapers.org/?a=d\&d=ndrs19530113-01.1.2\&e=-------en-20--1--txt-txIN----------). *Nassau Daily Review-Star*. 13 January 1953. Archived from [the original](https://www.nyshistoricnewspapers.org/?a=d\&d=ndrs19530113-01.1.2\&e=-------en-20--1--txt-txIN----------) on 6 October 2025. Retrieved 4 June 2025. WESTON, W. Va. 'Look what's going to happen here', said Donzel Raymond McCray as he dis-played five or six sticks of dyna-mite strapped to his waist. As five persons, including his divorced wife, looked on in horror yesterday, he touched two small batteries to wires extending from the dynamite. McCray was blown to bits and his wife and her lawyer, Charles N. Bland, were critically injured. The other three witnesses Magistrate W. S. Fults, Linn Mapel Brannon, and 78-year-old J. N. Osborn escaped serious injury. McCray, 47, and his wife were divorced last September. They had six children.

[^cite_note-Masada-93]: &#x20;Hassan, Riaz (2011). [*Suicide Bombings*](https://books.google.com/books?id=DgQ_opkHdJ0C\&q=Jewish++sect+suicidal\&pg=PA8). Taylor & Francis. p. 8. [ISBN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISBN_\(identifier\)) [978-1-136-80452-6](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special\:BookSources/978-1-136-80452-6). Retrieved 13 October 2015. \
    &#x20;Quote: "Members of these sects numbered in the hundreds and, starting around AD 48, carried out suicidal missions to kill prominent Jews, temple priests who had succumbed to Hellenistic culture and Roman soldiers … Zealots and Sicarii continued their attacks for a quarter of a century, provoking brutal Roman retaliatory reprisals … The Jewish War finally ended at Masada. When the Roman army attacked this fortress at the end of AD 72, there were 960 insurgents and refugees within. Once the fall of the fortress became inevitable, Eleazar, the leader of the Zealots, persuaded Masada's defenders to engage in what remains one of the most famous group suicides in history. The Zealots in Masada preferred to die by their own hand rather than be captured by their Roman enemies. The symbolic act demonstrated their steadfast opposition to Roman oppression. The act of mass suicide was a political act." Citing (Rapoport 1984: 670): "… To generate a mass uprising, they escalated the struggle by shock tactics to manipulate fear, outrage, sympathy and guilt. Sometimes these emotional effects were provoked by terrorist atrocities which went beyond the consensual norms governing violence; at other times, they were produced by provoking the enemy into committing atrocities against his will." Citing (Pape 2005: 34): "… to be able to die nobly and freely… Only our shared death is able to protect our wives and children from violation and slavery … We, who have been brought up at home in this way, should set an example to others in our readiness to die… This suicide is commanded by our laws. Our wives and children ask for it. God himself has sent us the necessity for it."

[^cite_note-unimaginable-94]: &#x20;["Australian man feared he'd have to do the unimaginable if Hamas found his children"](https://www.sbs.com.au/news/article/australian-man-feared-hed-have-to-do-the-unimaginable-if-hamas-found-his-children/gix186lxt). *[SBS News](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SBS_News)*. 13 October 2023. (Anthony lived) … with his Israeli-Australian wife and their three children aged under four lived in Kibbutz Be-eri … he thought he would have to make an impossible decision if Hamas fighters found them. 'The only thing going through my mind was, "If they come in here, I'm going to have to kill my kids myself",' Anthony said. … 'I didn't want my kids to become prisoners, so I thought I was going to have to kill my kids.' Scores of Israelis and others were taken to Gaza as hostages…

[^cite_note-JP_844045-95]: &#x20;["'All means are kosher': A look at the IDF's controversial Hannibal Directive - explainer"](https://web.archive.org/web/20250227184954/https://www.jpost.com/israel-news/article-844045). The Jerusalem Post. 27 February 2025. Archived from [the original](https://www.jpost.com/israel-news/article-844045) on 27 February 2025. The Hannibal Directive was reportedly created in the 1980s in response to the increased efforts by terrorist groups to take Israeli soldiers captive. These terrorist groups would use their hostages as bargaining chips in prisoner exchanges, where Israel would have to release a disproportionate amount of security prisoners in exchange for their captured soldiers.

[^cite_note-Freya_CBC-96]: &#x20;["Assessing The Nazi's 'Freya': The Secret Dieppe Mission"](https://web.archive.org/web/20250726143551/https://www.cbc.ca/xcompany/m_dispatches/assessing-freya-the-dieppe-secret-dieppe-mission). *[www.cbc.ca](http://www.cbc.ca/)*. [Canada](5042916.md). 5 April 2016. Archived from [the original](https://www.cbc.ca/xcompany/m_dispatches/assessing-freya-the-dieppe-secret-dieppe-mission) on 26 July 2025. … a true story about a Brit named [Jack Nissenthall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jack_Nissenthall). A radar expert for the [RAF](25679.md), Nissenthall and a contingent of 11 soldiers from the [South Saskatchewan Regiment](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/South_Saskatchewan_Regiment) were tasked with assaulting the Freya radar station and then, hopefully, learning its capabilities … Fearing what would happen if Nissenthall fell into enemy hands, he was given a [cyanide capsule](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cyanide_capsule) and, as added insurance, the men accompanying him had orders to kill him if capture was imminent … Nissenthall thought up a plan to cut all the German telephone lines around the radar station. This, he hoped, would force the Germans to broadcast their radar findings over the radio instead, giving Allied eavesdroppers a chance to listen in and gain valuable insights into Freya's capabilities … Miraculously, he then made it back to the beach where the Allied withdrawal was already underway. The casualties at Dieppe were severe. In just over six hours, over 1,500 Canadians were either killed or wounded, and another 2,000 were captured … Nissenthall would escape unscathed, but his Canadian escort was decimated, with only one man from the Saskatchewans returning alongside him … Nissenthall's efforts proved invaluable. His plan had worked, and the Allies intercepted a number of radio communications that helped assess Freya's strengths.

[^cite_note-JN_JC-97]: &#x20;Gilholy, Georgia L. (19 August 2022). ["How a young Jewish electronics expert helped win the War"](https://www.thejc.com/life/how-a-young-jewish-electronics-expert-helped-win-the-war-e257yvrr). *[The Jewish Chronicle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Jewish_Chronicle)*. His expertise was considered so crucial to the success of "[Operation Jubilee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Operation_Jubilee)" that he was accompanied by 11 bodyguards, Canadian soldiers, tasked not only with safeguarding him throughout the perilous assignment but with shooting him should they fall into enemy hands. He also carried a [cyanide pill](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cyanide_pill), just in case he needed to end his own life … More than half of the 6,086 men who landed as part of the operation were killed, injured, or captured within 10 hours, including ten of his 11 bodyguards. [Winston Churchill](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Winston_Churchill) maintained that the knowledge gained by the operation shaped the success of the subsequent [D-Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/D-Day) landings. Supreme Allied Commander [Lord Mountbatten](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lord_Mountbatten) later remarked of the raid, "For every man that died at Dieppe, ten were saved on [D-Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/D-Day)".

[^cite_note-tr_49008320-100]: &#x20;["Death Before Capture"](https://web.archive.org/web/20250604121741/https://trove.nla.gov.au/newspaper/article/49008320). *[The West Australian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_West_Australian)*. 2 January 1952. [^cite_note-101]: &#x20;["Suicide terrorism may have arrived in Australia"](https://web.archive.org/web/20151008014304/https://www.abc.net.au/news/2015-10-07/obrien-suicide-terrorism-may-have-arrived-in-australia/6833970). *ABC News*. 7 October 2015. However, the Collins dictionary describes a suicide attack as "a terrorist attack which someone undertakes knowing that he or she will die in the attack". I would argue that this definition more accurately describes a suicide attack.

[Chennai, India](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chennai,_India). Retrieved 28 August 2018. It is quite possible that Kuyili's act may have been one of the first instances of a suicide bombing in the history of mankind that ended up leaving not one speck of ammunition left for the British to retaliate.

[^cite_note-queen-velu-148]: &#x20; ["Remembering Queen Velu Nachiyar of Sivagangai, the first queen to fight the British"](http://www.thenewsminute.com/article/remembering-queen-velu-nachiyar-sivagangai-first-queen-fight-british-55163). *[The News Minute](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_News_Minute)*. 3 January 2017. In 1780, she led the charge against her enemies with an army she had built along with the help of Sultan Hyder Ali of Mysore. She is also credited to have been the first person to use a human bomb – Kuyili, her loyal follower (and some say adoptive daughter), doused herself in oil/ghee and set herself aflame to blow up the ammunition of the British.

[اكتشاف أثري ينسف حكاية تدمير "شمشون" لمعبد فلسطيني بساعديه – زلزال بقوة 8 درجات دك الهيكل قبل 2900 عام](https://www.alarabiya.net/articles/2010%2F07%2F29%2F115166) \[An archaeological discovery debunks the story of Samson destroying a Palestinian temple with his own hands. An 8.0 magnitude earthquake destroyed the temple 2,900 years ago.]. *alarabiya.net* (in Arabic). \[Samson the Mighty … destroyed a [Philistine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Philistine) temple with his own hands 2,900 years ago in ancient Gaza, where he stood and cried "[Against me and my enemies, O Lord!](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Die_with_the_Philistines)" before pushing two pillars in the temple, which collapsed and its stones fell on him and his Philistine enemies inside, thus marking the first suicide operation (عملية انتحارية) in history.]

[^cite_note-first_shahid-150]: &#x20;["שמשון היה השאהיד הראשון" שוב היהודים אשמים. העיתונות הערבית חשפה את ההשראה למחבלים המתאבדים: שמשון הגיבור היה המתאבד הראשון בהיסטוריה](https://www.makorrishon.co.il/nrg/online/1/ART1/586/982.html) \["Samson was the first shahid"] (in Hebrew). [Al-Ittihad (Emirati newspaper)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Ittihad_\(Emirati_newspaper\)). 27 May 2007 – via [Makor Rishon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Makor_Rishon) (with criticism). הכתבה, שפורסמה בעיתון "אל-אתחאד", היוצא לאור באיחוד האמירויות הערביות, תחת הכותרת "פיגועי ההתאבדות החלו ביהודים ולא הסתיימו בקמיקאזה", מתארת את פיגועי ההתאבדות לאורך ההיסטוריה, ומזכירה למשל את הטייסים היפנים ממלחמת העולם השנייה שצללו למותם כדי לפגוע באוניות אמריקניות, את ה"נמרים הטמילים" מסרי-לנקה, ואת המתאבדים הספרדים שפוצצו עצמם במהלך מלחמת האזרחים שפרצה במדינה בשנות ה-30 של המאה שעברה. \[The article, published in the newspaper "Al-Ittihad", published in the United Arab Emirates, under the headline "Suicide attacks began with Jews and did not end with kamikazes", describes suicide attacks throughout history, and mentions, for example, the Japanese pilots from World War II who dived to their deaths to hit American ships, the "Tamil Tigers" from Sri Lanka, and the Spanish suicide bombers who blew themselves up during the civil war that broke out in the country in the 1930s.] – Note: the Hebrew word used is "Shahid", which is derived from the Arabic word "Shaheed", meaning [martyr](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martyr), but which is not generally used to refer to Jewish martyrs. It has connotations of terrorism in [Modern Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Modern_Hebrew).

[^cite_note-AOAV_1881-164]: &#x20;["A short history of suicide bombing"](https://web.archive.org/web/20210502110011/https://aoav.org.uk/2020/a-short-history-of-suicide-bombings/). *aoav.org.uk*. United Kingdom: AOAV. 23 August 2020. Archived from [the original](https://aoav.org.uk/2020/a-short-history-of-suicide-bombings/) on 2 May 2021. Retrieved 6 October 2025. On 13 March 1881, Ignaty Grinevitsky watched as his accomplice threw a small bomb at the convoy of Tsar Alexander II outside the Winter Palace in St Petersburg. Safely enclosed in a carriage made from bullet-proof material as a gift from Napoleon III, the Tsar stepped out, dazed but unhurt.

[^cite_note-165]: &#x20;[Naimark, Norman M.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Norman_M._Naimark) (2006). ["Terrorism and the fall of Imperial Russia"](https://books.google.com/books?id=rjSjAUcH0vsC). In [Rapoport, David C.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_C._Rapoport) (ed.). *Terrorism: The first or anarchist wave*. Terrorism: Critical Concepts in Political Science. Vol. 1. Taylor & Francis. p. 280. [ISBN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISBN_\(identifier\)) [978-0-415-31651-4](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special\:BookSources/978-0-415-31651-4). Retrieved 17 April 2015. \[…] [Sof'ia Perovskaia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sophia_Perovskaya) \[…] and [Andrei Zheliabov](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andrei_Zhelyabov) carefully planned another attempt on the life of the Tsar. \[…] They rented a shop on Malaia Sadovaia, a street frequented by the Tsar, and dug a tunnel from the basement under the street. Zheliabov was arrested on 27 February 1881, and Perovskaia took charge of the assassination, planned for 1 March. This time they got their prey: the explosives placed under the street failed to detonate, but the second of two suicide bombers fatally wounded the Tsar.

[^cite_note-166]: &#x20;Julicher, Peter (2003). [*Renegades, Rebels and Rogues Under the Tsars*](https://books.google.com/books?id=cnN_x0ssn40C). McFarland. p. 229. [ISBN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISBN_\(identifier\)) [978-0-7864-1612-7](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special\:BookSources/978-0-7864-1612-7). When it swerved to avoid him, he threw his bomb through the side window. The explosion killed Plehve and left Sazonov badly injured.

[^cite_note-167]: &#x20; ["The Murchison tragedy"](https://natlib-primo.hosted.exlibrisgroup.com/primo-explore/fulldisplay?vid=NLNZ\&docid=INNZ7118417400002837\&context=L\&search_scope=INNZ). *natlib-primo.hosted.exlibrisgroup.com*. Journal (Murchison District Historical and Museum Society), Dec 1992; p.20-24

[^cite_note-168]: &#x20;"NZ suicide bombing a world first". *[The Press](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Press)*. 16 January 2016. p. A13. (access date: 17 January 2016)&#x20;

[^cite_note-169]: &#x20;Hindmarsh, Gerard (16 January 2016). ["Murchison suicide bombing a world first"](https://web.archive.org/web/20250922195911/https://www.stuff.co.nz/nelson-mail/opinion/75765195/murchison-suicide-bombing-a-world-first). *www.[stuff.co.nz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stuff.co.nz)*. Archived from [the original](http://www.stuff.co.nz/nelson-mail/opinion/75765195/murchison-suicide-bombing-a-world-first) on 22 September 2025. Retrieved 22 September 2025. the world's first strapped-on suicide bombing happened in Murchison. Joseph Sewell was a 57-year-old farmer from Longford when he strapped himself with sticks of gelignite and blew himself up outside the Murchison Courthouse on Friday 14 July 1905. It was an event that got reported worldwide as "The Murchison Tragedy".

[^cite_note-170]: &#x20; Gillespie, Alexander. ["Searching for our own Guy Fawkes"](https://web.archive.org/web/20250922174811/https://www.stuff.co.nz/opinion/98540798/searching-for-our-own-guy-fawkes). *www.[stuff.co.nz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stuff.co.nz)*. Archived from [the original](https://www.stuff.co.nz/opinion/98540798/searching-for-our-own-guy-fawkes) on 22 September 2025. New Zealand made its most significant contribution to the practice of terrorism on the global stage, providing the world's first suicide bomber in 1905 ... the first was Joseph Sewell, a 57-year-old farmer who strapped sticks of gelignite to himself and then detonated outside the Murchison court house ... Sewell attached the explosives to himself and then put his coat over the top ... Sewell only blew himself up, and only did it because of a dispute with his neighbour over the ownership of straying animals over unfenced lands, not to make some political point.

[^cite_note-171]: &#x20; ["JOURNAL: First blood / by Gerard Hindmarsh"](https://natlib.govt.nz/records/31940093?search%5Bi%5D%5Bsubject%5D=New+Zealand+--+Murchison\&search%5Bi%5D%5Btag%5D=natlib%3Aonly_at_the_library\&search%5Bpath%5D=items). *natlib.govt.nz*. Electronic title 'Feuds: first blood' ... Source: New Zealand listener (1994), 4 May 2013; v.238 n.3808: p.22-23 Gerard Hindmarsh. ["First blood"](https://natlib-primo.hosted.exlibrisgroup.com/primo-explore/fulldisplay?vid=NLNZ\&docid=INNZ7113888930002837\&context=L\&search_scope=INNZ). *Part Of: [New Zealand listener](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Zealand_Listener) (1994), 4 May 2013; v.238 n.3808\:p.22-23* – via natlib-primo.hosted.exlibrisgroup.com. Description: Revisits and details an incident at the Murchison courthouse 14 Jul 1905, in which accused man Joseph Sewell detonated an explosive that killed him and injured others. Claims it may be the first incident of suicide bombing using explosives strapped to one's body.

[^cite_note-Te_Ara_1-172]: &#x20;["Terrorism and New Zealand: the historical background"](https://teara.govt.nz/en/terrorism-and-counter-terrorism/page-1). *Te Ara Encyclopedia of New Zealand (teara.govt.nz)*. [New Zealand Government](8899161.md). 22 September 2025. p. 1. In modern times the definition of terrorism remains contested but is generally applied to actions that: — have political aims and motives  •  are violent or threaten violence  •  are designed to have far-reaching psychological repercussions beyond the immediate target  •  are conducted by an organisation with an identifiable structure. — Some people believe that the term terrorism applies only to actions carried out by groups, and not to the actions of countries or individuals. … The assassination of US President William McKinley in September 1901 by the anarchist Leon Czolgosz sparked calls in the New Zealand press to 'exterminate' anarchists. Assassins and anarchists were identified as 'foreigners' and 'continentals' – generally Italian, German or Russian. From the 1880s, police detectives were assigned to keep watch for the arrival of foreign revolutionaries ... New Zealand has been fortunate to have largely been free of terrorism. There have, however, been a number of incidents that might be seen as terrorist. Most involved the actions of individuals rather than organised groups, and lacked clear political motives. One such action was by farmer Joseph Sewell, who blew himself up outside the Murchison courthouse on 14 July 1905. Sewell was angered by a dispute with another local farmer over the ownership of a heifer. In some cases the actions were clearly aimed at property rather than people. It is therefore questionable how accurately they can be described as terrorism. … The [Wanganui Computer Centre bombing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wanganui_Computer_Centre_bombing): On the night of 18 November 1982, 22-year-old Neil Roberts was killed while attempting to blow up the police computer at the Wanganui Computer Centre. Roberts, an anarchist with a long involvement in the anti-racist and peace movements, appears to have intended to kill himself in the explosion. No one else was harmed in the attack, which targeted the computer rather than police personnel. Roberts's attack was aimed as a blow against what he saw as an oppressive state and society. He left the graffiti message: 'We have maintained a silence closely resembling stupidity'.`{{cite web}}`: CS1 maint: deprecated archival service ([link](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Category\:CS1_maint:_deprecated_archival_service))

Refusing to surrender, 300 Chinese troops blew them selves to pieces with their own hand-grenades. The self-sacrifice occurred, says a Shanghai message, in the Tientsin-Pukow conflict. The 300 were part of the army of 200,000 who are attempting to stem the Japanese advance. These men swore to die, rather than to surrender their positions. Their commander, Wang Ming-chang, blew out his brains with his revolver after sustaining a wound in the stomach. His men followed him with grenade.

Retrieved 21 December 2024. כאשר תוכננה התנקשות בגנרל בארקר, מפקד הצבא הבריטי בארץ־ישראל, הועלה רעיון, שבחורה תטייל עם עגלת תינוק, שתתפוצץ כאשר הגנרל יעבור לידה. נשאלה השאלה איך הבחורה תצליח להסתלק לפני ההתפוצצות. כאן התפרצה פניה והציעה את עצמה. בשביל פעולה כזאת, אמרה, היא תמצא כוחות לעמוד וללכת. ״ממילא חיי אינם חיים״, הוסיפה היא התכוונה לפעולת ״תמות נפשי עם פלשתים״. הצעתה נדחתה, כמובן. פניה רסקין הלכה לעולמה ב־-20.7.1947ג׳ מנחם אב תש״ז, בירושלים והיא בת 31`{{cite book}}`: CS1 maint: location missing publisher ([link](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Category\:CS1_maint:_location_missing_publisher)) ["Raskin, Fania – Freedom Fighters of Israel Heritage Association"](https://web.archive.org/web/20220707020736/https://lehi.org.il/en/raskin-fania/). Archived from [the original](https://lehi.org.il/en/raskin-fania/) on 7 July 2022. Fania then spoke up and volunteered herself. She was sure that for an operation such as this, she would find the strength to stand and walk. "My life is no life anyway" she added. This was a "[Let my soul die with the Philistines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Let_my_soul_die_with_the_Philistines)" proposal, the Samson option. Of course, her suggestion was rejected. Fania Raskin passed away on 20 July 1947, in Jerusalem. She was thirty-one. [רסקין פַניה – "מרגלית" – העמותה להנצחת מורשת לח״י](https://lehi.org.il/he/%d7%a8%d7%a1%d7%a7%d7%99%d7%9f-%d7%a4%d6%b7%d7%a0%d7%99%d7%94/) (in Hebrew). הנצחה באנדרטה ביער לח"י במשמר איילון … שמה של פניה רסקין חרוט על לוח באנדרטה ביער לח"י \[Commemoration at the monument in the Lehi Forest in Mishmar Ayalon … Fania Raskin's name is engraved on a plaque at the Lehi Forest memorial.] – Description: In the close up image, her name is the last on the list [Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): פניה רסקין, [romanized](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romanization_of_Hebrew): *Fania Raskin*, with her Hebrew alias in brackets [Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): מרגלית, [romanized](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romanization_of_Hebrew): *Margalit*.

(Note: "today in history" refers to the anniversary in the Hebrew calendar) Quote: [Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): תמות נפשי עם פלשתים — ברזני ופינשטיין עירקי ואשכנזי ישבו ביחד בתא הנידונים למוות וחיכו בשלווה ליום מותם. איש לח"י אליעזר בן עמי העלה את רעיון שמשון תמות נפשי עם בריטים. והכין לשניים תפוז נפץ כדי שביום התלייה גם התליינים יתפוצצו., [lit.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Literal_translation) 'My soul will die with the Philistines — Barzani and Feinstein, Iraqi and Ashkenazi, sat together in the death row cell and calmly awaited the day of their death. A Lehi member, [Eliezer Ben Ami](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eliezer_Ben_Ami?action=edit\&redlink=1), came up with the idea of Samson dying with the British. And he prepared an explosive orange for the two of them so that on the day of the hanging, the executioners would also explode.'

[^cite_note-Perth_22_April_1947-245]: &#x20;["Terrorists Suicide Before Execution"](https://trove.nla.gov.au/newspaper/article/78209995). *[Daily News (Perth, Western Australia)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daily_News_\(Perth,_Western_Australia\))*. [Perth, Western Australia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Perth,_Western_Australia). 22 April 1947. JERUSALEM, Tuesday — Two Jewish terrorists who were to have been executed before dawn today committed suicide in their cells shortly before midnight last night. They killed themselves by holding small quantities of explosives against their hearts shortly after they had received the last rites from. Jewish prison chaplain Rabbi [Jacob Goldman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jacob_Goldman). It is not known yet how they detonated the explosive. Rabbi Goldman was taken to police headquarters immediately after the men suicided. The men inscribed the Biblical phrase '[Mene mene tekel upharsin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mene_mene_tekel_upharsin)' on the walls of their cells in the Jerusalem central prison be- fore killing themselves. The phrase is from the [book of Daniel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Book_of_Daniel), [chapter 5](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniel_5). verse 25. Daniel gave the interpretation of mene as *God hath numbered thy kingdom and finished it* and tekel as *Thou art weighed in the balances and art found wanting.* The two men, Meyer Feinstein and Moshe Barazani, had earlier appealed to the High Commissioner for clemency. Feinstein was sentenced to death for his part in the bomb outrage at [Jerusalem railway station](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jerusalem%E2%80%93Khan_railway_station) last October and Barazani was sentenced for having a hand grenade in his possession last month.

[ירושלים (מאיר פיינשטיין ומשה ברזני)](https://db.begincenter.org.il/article/%D7%99%D7%A8%D7%95%D7%A9%D7%9C%D7%99%D7%9D-%D7%9E%D7%90%D7%99%D7%A8-%D7%A4%D7%99%D7%99%D7%A0%D7%A9%D7%98%D7%99%D7%99%D7%9F-%D7%95%D7%9E%D7%A9%D7%94-%D7%91%D7%A8%D7%96%D7%A0%D7%99/). *מאגר כתבי מנחם בגין - מרכז מורשת בגין* (in Hebrew).

[^cite_note-Begin_1951-250]: &#x20;[Begin, Menachem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Menachem_Begin). [*The Revolt*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Revolt) (English revised ed.). [Dell Books](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dell_Books). ["Archived"](https://archive.org/details/the-revolt-menahem-begin). *archive.org*. 26 December 2021.

[^cite_note-BN_2010-251]: &#x20;[Netanyahu](56472.md) (9 March 2010). ["PM Netanyahu's Speech at the Knesset Special Session in Memory of the Jewish Prisoners Hung from the Gallows"](https://www.gov.il/en/pages/speechgardom090310). *www\.gov.il/en*. Which of us does not remember the almost unbelievable story of [Moshe Barazani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moshe_Barazani) and Meir Feinstein? Grenades were smuggled to their prison cells in a basket of oranges. Their original plan was to blow themselves up together as they were being hung. However, it then became clear to them that one of the rabbis of the Jewish community intended to be there as they were executed. In order not to endanger him, the two decided that after his visit, they would take their fate into their own hands. They embraced firmly and exploded the grenades up against their hearts. It is doubtful that anyone could invent such an unbelievable story, a story which expresses supreme heroism, sublime love for country and people. Yes, love of country, love of the homeland, love for their people.

[^cite_note-Chissin-253]: &#x20;**Hebrew version:** [שם: בן־עמי (חסין) אליעזר – כינוי בלח״י: יחזקאל](https://web.archive.org/web/20230130084546/https://lehi.org.il/he/%D7%91%D7%9F%D6%BE%D7%A2%D7%9E%D7%99-%D7%97%D7%A1%D7%99%D7%9F-%D7%90%D7%9C%D7%99%D7%A2%D7%96%D7%A8-%D7%99%D7%97%D7%96%D7%A7%D7%90%D7%9C/) \[Name: Ben-Ami (Chissin) Eliezer – Nickname in Lehi: Yehezkel]. *lehi.org.il/he* (in Hebrew). Archived from [the original](https://web.archivhttps//lehi.org.il/he/%D7%91%D7%9F%D6%BE%D7%A2%D7%9E%D7%99-%D7%97%D7%A1%D7%99%D7%9F-%D7%90%D7%9C%D7%99%D7%A2%D7%96%D7%A8-%D7%99%D7%97%D7%96%D7%A7%D7%90%D7%9C/) on 30 January 2023. בין תפקידיו המרובים: מדריך חבלה, סילוק פצצות, קצין ייצור, מפקד סדנא, ראש מדור ניסויים, מג"ד 276 ורע"נ אב"כ. (ראש ענף אטומית ביולוגית כימית). ביום 20 באפריל 1948 נשא אליעזר לאישה את חברתו מהלח"י דרורה גלעדי (רזניצקי), אותה הכיר בשנת 1943. נולדו להם: ארנון, דורון ורווית, ונכדים. \[Among his many positions: sabotage instructor, bomb disposal, production officer, workshop commander, head of the experimental section, battalion commander 276 and senior engineer in A.B.C. (head of the Atomic Biological Chemical Branch). On 20 April 1948, Eliezer married his girlfriend from Lehi, Drora Giladi (Reznitzky), whom he had met in 1943.] **Translation note:** In Hebrew it uses the acronyms [Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): רע"נ אב"כ, [romanized](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romanization_of_Hebrew): *[Resh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Resh).[Ayn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ayin).[Nun](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nun_\(letter\)). [Aleph](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aleph).[Bet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bet_\(letter\)).[Kaph](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kaph).* then expands them in brackets as [Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): ראש ענף אטומית ביולוגית כימית, [lit.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Literal_translation) 'Branch head Atomic Biological Chemical'. **English translation:** ["Ben-Ami (Chissin) Eliezer – "Yechezkel" – Freedom Fighters of Israel Heritage Association"](https://lehi.org.il/en/ben-ami-chassin-eliezer/). *lehi.org.il/en*. He became known for assembling 'Grenade Filled Oranges' used by [Moshe Barazani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moshe_Barazani) and [Meir Feinstein](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Meir_Feinstein), who blew themselves up three hours before their British-planned execution. He was released 8 February 1948, and stayed active until joining the [IDF](39237.md) 31 May 1948. He was discharged September 1969 ranking Lieutenant-Colonel. Eliezer served in the 8th Brigade, the Engineering Corps, and the Ordnance Corps, from dismantling bombshells to Commanding a Battalion. In 1951 he successfully finished an Engineer Corps Officers' Course and was sent to study at the Technion. He was involved in developing and testing armaments. He graduated Command and HQ School. Among his positions: instructor, sapper, Production Officer, Experimental Branch Head, 276 Battalion Commander, and Head of the Atomic-Biological-Chemical Weapons Branch.

[^cite_note-Reznitsky_(he)-254]: &#x20;אלבז-אלוש, קורין (24 May 2023). [חיל החלוץ: צה"ל חוגג 75, ואלו שהתגייסו עם הקמתו זוכרים איך הכול התחיל](https://web.archive.org/web/20230526182429/https://www.ynet.co.il/news/article/r1oxgsqhh) \[Pioneer Corps: The IDF celebrates 75 years, and those who enlisted when it was founded remember how it all began]. *[Ynet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ynet)* (in Hebrew). Archived from [the original](https://www.ynet.co.il/news/article/r1oxgsqhh) on 26 May 2023. … "התגייסתי עם שם בדוי … בחרתי להתגייס כאליעזר רזניצקי. לקחתי את שם משפחתה של חברתי, שחיכתה לי במשך חמש שנים כשישבתי בכלא ולימים הפכה לאשתי. ידעתי שמפא"י לא רוצה את גבולות הארץ שאני רציתי ושצה"ל לא יהיה צבא השחרור של עם ישראל, אז פעלתי כמו במחתרת, שבה היו לנו כינויים … רק כשקיבלתי דרגת סמל חזרתי לשם משפחתי המקורי" … בן עמי התגייס לחטיבה 8, חטיבת השריון הראשונה בפיקודו של יצחק שדה. משם עבר לחיל ההנדסה, ואז לחימוש, ושוב להנדסה. הוא יצא לקורס קציני ההנדסה הראשון, סיים בהצטיינות והמשיך לשלל תפקידים: מסלק פצצות, מדריך חבלה וראש ענף אב"כ. \[… 'I enlisted under a false name … I chose to enlist as Eliezer Reznitzky. I took the last name of my girlfriend, who waited for me for five years while I was in prison and later became my wife. I knew that [Mapai](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mapai) did not want the borders of the country that I wanted and that the IDF would not be the liberation army of the people of Israel, so I acted like I did in the underground, where we had nicknames … Only when I received the rank of sergeant did I return to my original last name' … Ben Ami enlisted in the 8th Brigade, the first armored brigade under the command of [Yitzhak Sadeh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yitzhak_Sadeh). From there he transferred to the Engineering Corps, then to Armaments, and back to Engineering. He took the first Engineering Officers' Course, graduated with honors, and went on to hold a variety of positions: bomb disposal officer, sabotage instructor, and head of the ABC branch.] — **Note:** The name is [Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): אליעזר רזניצקי, [romanized](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romanization_of_Hebrew): *Eliezer Reznitsky*, and the acronym is [Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): אב"כ, [romanized](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romanization_of_Hebrew): *A.B.C.* with the letters [Aleph](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aleph)–[Bet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bet_\(letter\))–[Kaph](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kaph), which stands for "atomic, biological, and chemical".

[^cite_note-srugim_obt-255]: &#x20;[ברוך דיין האמת: נפטר לוחם לח"י אליעזר בן עמי](https://www.srugim.co.il/829766-%d7%91%d7%a8%d7%95%d7%9a-%d7%93%d7%99%d7%99%d7%9f-%d7%94%d7%90%d7%9e%d7%aa-%d7%a0%d7%a4%d7%98%d7%a8-%d7%9c%d7%95%d7%97%d7%9d-%d7%9c%d7%97%d7%99-%d7%90%d7%9c%d7%99%d7%a2%d7%96%d7%a8-%d7%91%d7%9f). סרוגים \[*srugim.co.il*] (in Hebrew). 15 August 2023. לאחר הקמת המדינה התגייס לצה"ל ושירת בחיל ההנדסה ובחיל החימוש, ובין תפקידו השונים הוא שימש מדריך חבלה, סילוק פצצות, קצין ייצור, מפקד סדנא, ראש מדור ניסויים, מג"ד 276 ורע"נ אב"כ. ([Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): רע"נ אב"כ, [lit.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Literal_translation) 'head of [WMDs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/WMDs)')

[^cite_note-EBA_SA-256]: &#x20;[לוחמים / אליעזר "יחזקאל" בן עמי (לח"י)](https://web.archive.org/web/20250513093014/https://shayaloni.co.il/portfolio/%D7%90%D7%9C%D7%99%D7%A2%D7%96%D7%A8-%D7%91%D7%9F-%D7%A2%D7%9E%D7%99/) \[Fighters / Eliezer "Yehezkel" Ben Ami (Lehi)]. *שי אלוני* שי אלוני shayaloni.co.il (in Hebrew). Archived from [the original](https://shayaloni.co.il/portfolio/%D7%90%D7%9C%D7%99%D7%A2%D7%96%D7%A8-%D7%91%D7%9F-%D7%A2%D7%9E%D7%99/) on 13 May 2025. בן עמי היה זה שתכנן ויצר את שני "רימוני התפוז" בתוך כתלי בית הכלא, עבור משה ברזני (לח"י) ומאיר פיינשטין (אצ"ל), שפוצצו עצמם למוות כשלוש שעות לפני הוצאתם לגרדום. ב 1948, לאחר שחרורו מהכלא שב לפעילות בלח"י עד לפרוק המחתרת. בן עמי התגייס לצה"ל לחטיבה 8 עם כל לוחמי לח"י, בהמשך עבר להנדסה ולחיל החימוש. עסק בפיתוח אמצעי לחימה. בוגר ביה"ס לפיקוד ומטה. בין תפקידיו הרבים בצבא: מדריך חבלה וסילוק פצצות, קצין יצור, מפקד סדנא, ראש מדור ניסויים, מג"ד 276 וראש ענף לוחמת אב"כ. ב 1969, השתחרר מ צה"ל בדרגת סא"ל.

[^cite_note-Reznitsky_(en)-259]: &#x20;Elbaz Alush, Korin (25 May 2023). ["In honor of IDF's 75th anniversary, first recruits share stories from the frontlines"](https://www.ynetnews.com/magazine/article/sjb311f3rh). *Ynetnews*. Ben-Ami was quite the character, and knew no fear. Even when he joined the army, he acted on his own terms. "I enlisted under a false name," he said. "I chose to enlist as Eliezer Reznitsky. I took the last name of my girlfriend, who waited for me for five years while I was in prison, and later she became my wife. I knew that the \[Ben-Gurion's] [Mapai Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mapai_Party) didn't want the borders I wanted for the country, and that the IDF wouldn't be the liberation army of the Israeli nation. So, I acted as I did underground, where we had nicknames. I changed my last name so that I wouldn't be recognized, in case I needed to fight beyond the borders that the IDF set. Only when I received the rank of sergeant did I return to my original last name." Ben-Ami enlisted in the 8th Armored Brigade … he transferred to the Combat Engineering Corps, then to the Technology and Maintenance Corps, and then back to engineering. He enlisted in the first Engineering Officers Course, in which he excelled, and went on to fulfill various roles: sapper, explosives instructor, and also higher-ranking roles. (Note, this is an English version, not a direct translation of the story in Hebrew edition)

[^cite_note-19_October_2005-260]: &#x20;[החדר האטום הראשון](https://web.archive.org/web/20250513090810/https://www.haaretz.co.il/misc/2005-10-19/ty-article/0000017f-def9-d3ff-a7ff-fff906dc0000). *Haaretz הארץ – Hebrew edition* (in Hebrew). 19 October 2005. Archived from [the original](https://www.haaretz.co.il/misc/2005-10-19/ty-article/0000017f-def9-d3ff-a7ff-fff906dc0000) on 13 May 2025.

[^cite_note-1964_quote-262]: &#x20;[Begin 1964](#CITEREFBegin1964): [Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): והשניים בהם, מאיר פינשטיין, שאבותיו באו מאירופה, ומשה ברזני, שהוריו באו מבבל, עשו מעשה שלא היה כמוהו במלחמות השחרור. משפחתם הלוחמת עשתה כל מאמץ כדי להצילם מידי התליין, אבל ביום אחד הוברר כי לא תשיג עוד ידם להוציאם מידיו. שני לוחמים אלה, האחד מן האצ״ל והשני מן הלח״י, האחד אשכנזי והשני ספרדי, החליטו שלא יפלו בידי התליין, אלא גם במותם יילחמו, וכך עם רימון מוסתר בבגדיהם ילכו אל מקום ההוצאה להורג ויפעילוהו בנוכחות כל התליינים. בא אליהם הרב וביקש מהם שירשו לו ללוותם לגרדום, אך הם, ביודעם את רצונם, את תכניתם ואת שאיפתם, התחננו לפניו שלא יבוא בבוקר השכם ללכת עמם בדרך האחרונה. הוא עמד על שלו והם על שלהם. ומשנפרד מהם, לאחר ששלושתם שרו את האדיר בשירי התהילה — ״אדון עולם אשר מלך בטרם כל יציר נברא״ — והתייחדו עם בוראם, כדי לא לפגוע בו, ברב, בעת ההוצאה להורג, לחצו בין לבותיהם את רימון היד, וכך גמרו חייהם בתא המוות. חיבוק אחים כזה של שני יהודים צעירים, של שני לוחמים, אשר בגלל מורשת הגולה נדבק בהם עדיין התו המפריד של אשכנז וספרד — זהו הסמל המובהק ביותר והמקודש ביותר של אהבת ישראל, ללא הבדל מוצא וללא הבדל עדה.

[Eliezer Ben-Ami](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eliezer_Ben-Ami?action=edit\&redlink=1), an imprisoned Lehi member, assembled hand grenades from pieces that were smuggled into the prison separately…

[^cite_note-Saada_Karameh-290]: &#x20;[Saada & Merrill 2008](#CITEREFSaadaMerrill2008), pp. 18–20 "This was the same IDF that had so humiliated the Arab armies nine months before in the infamous Six-Day War. We Palestinians had been peppering them ever since with hitand-run attacks—a grenade here, a three-minute skirmish there. Now they had decided to storm our training camp at al-Karameh in force. They wanted to take out our operation wholesale, and maybe even get our heroic leader "Abu Ammar"—Yasser Arafat—in the process. … They had no idea that the wily Arafat had switched strategies this time, saying to us, "We will make a stand in this place. We will fight with honor. The whole of the Arab people are watching us. We will crush the myth that the IDF is invincible!" And they certainly did not expect the newest tactic we would use today for the very first time: suicide bombers. We had gotten volunteers who were willing to make this their final battle for Palestinian justice. They now waited on rooftops in their bulky vests loaded with explosives until the moment came to jump into the streets below." … "We Fatah fighters were in fact more agile than the IDF since we carried less gear than they did and could therefore run faster. We also had our bayonets already fixed on our weapons and ready to use while the Israelis were still fumbling to get theirs off their belts and attached. At times they literally ran into our knives. Whenever they tried to regroup behind one of their tanks, a suicide bomber would leap down from a rooftop with a thunderous explosion of nails and other metal bits. Blood spattered, and body parts flew through the air." — [full text on dokumen.pub](https://dokumen.pub/ich-kmpfte-fr-arafat-ein-fatah-heckenschtze-beginnt-ein-ganz-neues-leben-9783765514081-9781414323619-9781414327495-9781414327501-9781414327518-376551408x.html)

[^cite_note-1981_IAJBF-306]: &#x20;[Begin 1981](#CITEREFBegin1981): [Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): המפקד הגדול במחוזות היה תימני; עוזי היה ספרדי; גידי, שעשה את הפעולה ההיסטורית של מלון המלך דויד, היה ספרדי; הממונה על כל האסירים בלטרון היה תימני - וכל הבחורים שלנו עמדו דום לפניו. איזו בעיה? אין לנו. כולנו יהודים. כולנו אחים. כולנו לוחמים … יש ביניהם עולי גרדום, אשר עד הרגע האחרון לחייהם שרו את שיר ״התקווה״ והפליאו עולם ומלואו בגבורתם המופתית. והם הלכו לבתי־הסוהר, למחנות ריכוז, הם נלחמו ולא נשברו, הם זעקו בפני השופטים הבריטים את המילים ״אנחנו לא מכירים בלל בשלטונכם - עליכם להסתלק מכאן, מארץ־ישראל״. פיינשטיין היה ממוצא אירופאי, איך קוראים לזה, אשכנזי; משה ברזני היה ספרדי מעירק. בלילה לאחר שנידונו למוות, והיו צריכים בבוקר־בבוקר להוריד אותם מן התלייה, והרב היה איש זקן, הוא אמד שיבוא להוריד אותם, ולא רצו לפגוע ברב, הם לקחו ללבותיהם רימון יד, לחצו. אשכנזים? עירקים? יהודים. אחים. לוחמים.

[^cite_note-Waxman-2011-382]: &#x20;Waxman, Dov (2011). ["Living with terror, not Living in Terror: The Impact of Chronic Terrorism on Israeli Society"](https://web.archive.org/web/20120324132359/http://www.terrorismanalysts.com/pt/index.php/pot/article/view/living-with-terror/html). *Perspectives on Terrorism*. **5** (5–6). Archived from the original on 24 March 2012. Retrieved 14 October 2015. Palestinian terrorism during the second Intifada clearly affected the political preferences of the Israeli electorate. Sharon's resounding victory in the 2001 election was one indication of this effect. Another was the Likud party's decisive win in the 2003 Knesset elections, doubling the number of its seats in parliament (from 19 to 38), while the rival pro-negotiation center-left Labor party lost seven seats (dropping from 26 to 19 seats). Not only did Palestinian terrorism boost the electoral appeal of the political right in Israel, it also helped to bring about a rightward shift in the political positions of the Israeli public. In general, more Israelis identified themselves as right-wing and fewer as left-wing. ... Palestinian terrorism ... had a major impact on their attitudes towards the use of force against Palestinians. Israeli Jews became much more militant and 'hawkish.' ... Angry and embittered by the seemingly endless series of gruesome Palestinian suicide bombings inside Israel, the vast majority of the Israeli public staunchly supported the Sharon government's offensive military measures against the Palestinians. In 2001, for instance, 89 percent of Israeli Jews supported the Sharon government's policy of "targeted assassinations" of Palestinian militants involved in terrorism against Israel

[^cite_note-JTA_18_Apr_47-460]: &#x20;["Palestine Military Commander Orders Two More Jews to Hang; Commutes Sentence of Third"](https://www.jta.org/archive/palestine-military-commander-orders-two-more-jews-to-hang-commutes-sentence-of-third). *[Jewish Telegraphic Agency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_Telegraphic_Agency)*. 18 April 1947. Lt. Gen. [Gordon H.A. MacMillan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gordon_MacMillan), Palestine military commander, today confirmed the death sentences imposed on two Palestine Jews and commuted the sentence of a third to life imprisonment. Under amendments to the Emrgency Defense Regulations published yesterday there is no appeal from his decision. The condemned men are [Moshe Barazani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moshe_Barazani), 21, and [Meir Feinstein](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Meir_Feinstein), whose age the prosecution claims is 23. The third youth is [Daniel Azulai](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniel_Azulai?action=edit\&redlink=1). Barazani is a self-confessed [Sternist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avraham_Stern), who told the military court which convicted him that "you will not frighten us with your gallows". Feinstein's mother insists that her son, a veteran of the [British Army](4887.md), is only 17. Barazani was captured in Jerusalem during the [imposition of martial law](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/March_1947_martial_law_in_Mandatory_Palestine) with hand grenade in his possession. The other two youths were charged with having participated in an [attack on the Jerusalem central railroad station](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Attack_on_the_Jerusalem_central_railroad_station?action=edit\&redlink=1) last October.

[^cite_note-JTA_4_April_1947-461]: &#x20;["Two More Palestinian Jews Sentenced to Death; Seven Now Stand in Shadow of Gallows"](https://web.archive.org/web/20250511225304/https://www.jta.org/archive/two-more-palestinian-jews-sentenced-to-death-seven-now-stand-in-shadow-of-gallows). *[Jewish Telegraphic Agency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_Telegraphic_Agency)*. 4 April 1947. Archived from [the original](https://www.jta.org/archive/two-more-palestinian-jews-sentenced-to-death-seven-now-stand-in-shadow-of-gallows) on 11 May 2025. Feinstein was sentenced to death following a government "expert's" testimony that he was at least 18 years old. The death sentence may not be imposed on anybody under that age. His brother attempted several times to tell the court that he was only 17, but he was refused permission to testify. Since the defendant's birth certificate was missing, the "expert" was ordered to estimate his age.

[^cite_note-Benjamin-462]: &#x20;Feinstein, Benjamin (1 April 1947). ["Letter to: The President, Military Court Jerusalem"](https://web.archive.org/web/20250511212638/https://www.infocenters.co.il/jabo/jabo_multimedia/Files/linked/%D7%9713%20-3_43.PDF) (PDF). *[www.infocenters.co.il](http://www.infocenters.co.il/)*. Karem Quarter, Jerusalem. p. 10. Archived from [the original](https://www.infocenters.co.il/jabo/jabo_multimedia/Files/linked/%D7%9713%20-3_43.PDF) (PDF) on 11 May 2025. Now regarding the age of my brother Meir. So far the Prosecutors has tendered the following evidence on the subject: 1. A Birth Certificate issued by the Health Department, Government of Palestine, stating that the age of my brother Meir is 17 years 8 months. 2. My mother's sworn affidavit to the same effect. Medical Report by the Government Medical Officer, who was instructed to examine my brother at the request of the prosecutor, stating that my brother's age is 17½ yearв. Evidence by two military doctors that the age of my brother is 23-26 years.

[^cite_note-Bella-463]: &#x20;Feinstein, Bella (April 1947). ["SWORN by the said Mrs. BELLA FEINSTEIN of Jerusalem, before mе, Magistrete at Jerusalem this day of April 1947"](https://web.archive.org/web/20241230172926/https://www.infocenters.co.il/jabo/jabo_multimedia/Files/linked/%D7%9713%20-3_42.PDF) (PDF). *www\.infocenters.co.il/jabo/jabo_multimedia*. p. 5. Archived from [the original](https://www.infocenters.co.il/jabo/jabo_multimedia/Files/linked/%D7%9713%20-3_42.PDF) (PDF) on 30 December 2024. This affidavit is sworn in support of an application for a judgment declaring that my said son Meir was born in Jerusalem on the 30th day of July 1929, or alternatively that on the 3rd day of April, 1947 he had not yet attained the age of 18 years.

[^cite_note-Yehuda_Lapidot_-_the_Irgun-464]: &#x20;["Yehuda Lapidot - the Irgun"](https://archive.org/stream/yehuda-lapidot-the-irgun/Yehuda%20Lapidot%20-%20The%20Irgun_djvu.txt).

[^cite_note-Robert_Baer-465]: &#x20;["The making of a suicide bomber"](https://web.archive.org/web/20110523053003/http://www.timesonline.co.uk/tol/news/article626388.ece). *[The Times](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Times)*. [London](17867.md). 3 September 2006. Archived from [the original](http://www.timesonline.co.uk/tol/news/article626388.ece) on 23 May 2011. Retrieved 5 May 2010. Former [CIA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CIA_activities_in_Iran) officer [Robert Baer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_Baer) … "The origins of suicide bombing lie among the Shi'ite in Iran. A 13-year-old child, [Hossein Fahmideh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hossein_Fahmideh), strapped rocket-propelled grenades to his chest and blew himself up under an Iraqi tank in November 1980. [Ayatollah Khomeini](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ayatollah_Khomeini)'s embattled Islamic republic adopted Fahmideh as a national hero and as an inspiration for further bloodshed and martyrdom".

[^cite_note-tebyan_Mohammed_Hossein_Fahmideh-466]: &#x20;["Our leader: Mohammed Hossein Fahmideh"](https://web.archive.org/web/20110108160222/https://www.tebyan.net/index.aspx?pid=52570). *tebyan.net [Tebyan Cultural Institute](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tebyan_Cultural_Institute)*. 29 October 2007. Archived from [the original](http://www.tebyan.net/index.aspx?pid=52570) on 8 January 2011. Mohammed Hossein Fahmideh, was a 13 year old boy who is praised by Iranians as a true patriot… Hossein knew that his time would be up sooner or later; therefore he grabbed a hand grenade off one of the nearby bodies and pulled the pin out as he ran and jumped underneath an enemy tank, killing himself and disabling the tank … Ayatollah Khomeini declared Fahmideh Iranian national hero, calling him 'our leader'…
